}

    content: '✔';
    position: absolute;

    left: 3px;

    color: #f08d1b;
    line-height: 1.75;
    -webkit-transition: all .2s;
    -moz-transition: all .2s;
    -ms-transition: all .2s;
    transition: all .2s;
}

/* checked mark aspect changes */

    opacity: 0;
    -webkit-transform: scale(0);
    -moz-transform: scale(0);
    -ms-transform: scale(0);
    transform: scale(0);
}

    opacity: 1;
    -webkit-transform: scale(1);
    -moz-transform: scale(1);
    -ms-transform: scale(1);
    transform: scale(1);
}

    box-shadow: none;
    border-color: #bbb;
    background-color: #ddd;
}

    color: #999;
}

#formulaire_connexion [type="checkbox"]:disabled + label {
    color: #aaa;
}

<h2 class="titre_bloc_certification"><span class="stitre_bloc_certification">AFNOR Certification</span></h2>
<p>La collecte, modération et restitution des avis consommateurs traités par Avis Vérifiés se conforment à la norme NF Z74-501 et aux règles de certification NF522.</p>
<p>Cette certification par AFNOR Certification montre qu'Avis Vérifiés s'engage à rassurer les internautes sur l'authenticité des avis en ligne.</p>

<!-- Animations -->
<script>
	$(document).ready(function() {
		// Animations nuages logos
		function nuageLogos(timer) {
			var nuage = $('.nuage:not(.show)').first();
			if(nuage.length == 0){ clearInterval(timer); }
			$(nuage).show().addClass('show');
		}
		$('.bloc_nuage').appear();
		var appearNuages = false;
		$(document.body).on('appear', '.bloc_nuage', function(e, $affected) {
			if(appearNuages == false) {
				appearNuages = true;
				var timer;
				timer = setInterval(function(){ nuageLogos(timer); }, 100);
			}
		});
		// Animations Schéma Bénéfices
		function AffichageBenefices(timerBenefices) {
			var nuage = $('.grille_benefices li:not(.show)').first();
			if(nuage.length == 0){ clearInterval(timerBenefices); }
			$(nuage).show().addClass('show');
		}

		$('.grille_benefices').appear();

                if ($(window).height() > 700){
                    var timerBenefices;
                    timerBenefices = setInterval(function(){ AffichageBenefices(timerBenefices); }, 300);
                }else{
                    var appearBenefices = false;
                    $(document.body).on('appear', '.grille_benefices', function(e, $affected) {
                            if(appearBenefices == false) {
                                    appearBenefices = true;
                                    var timerBenefices;
                                    timerBenefices = setInterval(function(){ AffichageBenefices(timerBenefices); }, 300);
                            }
                    });
                }
	});
    </script>

<!-- Pie Charts statistiques -->
<script>
		$(document).ready(function() {
			$('.chart').appear();
			var appearCharts = false;
			$(document.body).on('appear', '.chart', function(e, $affected) {
				if(appearCharts == false) {
					appearCharts = true;
					setTimeout($('.chart').easyPieChart({
						easing: 'easeOutBounce',
						barColor: '#f39712',
						trackColor: false,
						scaleColor: false,
						size: 126,
						lineWidth: 4,
						animate: 3000,
						onStep: function(from, to, percent) {
							$(this.el).find('.percent').text(Math.round(percent));
						}
					}), 2000);
				}
			});
		});
	</script>
